// Entry decisions (plan stage 2, invariant I3): resolveCheckoutEntry (gate) where the gate
// is the process, frameworkOwner (installed command hand-over) everywhere, the installed
// entry's own placement decisions (resolveInstalledEntry, plus missingAppDecision where
// app.ts is absent), and the stray app.ts predicate. Fake fs, no processes. Paths are
// fake-root relative.

== platform=linux layout=checkout-root argv=<none> env=none
gate: run app=openclaw fact=openclaw/default argv=[] prefix=./clawforge
handover: spawn checkout-gate delegated=false argv=[]
stray: none

== o3 empty OC_APP
(a) destroy, empty OC_APP: refuse: OC_APP is set but empty — unset it or name a deployment
(b) help, empty OC_APP: run app=openclaw fact=openclaw/default argv=["help"] prefix=./clawforge
(b) bare, empty OC_APP: run app=openclaw fact=openclaw/default argv=[] prefix=./clawforge
(c) control, unset OC_APP: run app=openclaw fact=openclaw/default argv=["destroy"] prefix=./clawforge
